f}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}}-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}}|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}}-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}}|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}}-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]}}|}}

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{\epsilon>0}{\mathchoice{\left(4\eta mNL\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}\right)}{(4\eta mNL\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}})}{(4\eta mNL\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}})}{(4\eta mNL\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}})}}.

Proof.

Define
Z_{h}={\mathchoice{\left|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]\right|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]|}}
. For any
h_{1},h_{2}\in\mathcal{H}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left|Z_{h_{1}}-Z_{h_{2}}\right|}{|Z_{h_{1}}-Z_{h_{2}}|}{|Z_{h_{1}}-Z_{h_{2}}|}{|Z_{h_{1}}-Z_{h_{2}}|}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\lvert\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}

\displaystyle\quad-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}\Bigg\rvert

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{\mathchoice{\left|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}\right|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}|}{|\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}|}}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathchoice{\left|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}\right|}{|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}|}{|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}|}{|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))]}}|}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}]}}

\displaystyle\quad+\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}\right]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}]}{[\sum_{\bm{a}}{\mathchoice{\left|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))\right|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}{|(\tilde{b}(h_{1}(x),\bm{a})-\tilde{b}(h_{2}(x),\bm{a}))|}}]}}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}2\eta mNL(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}}+\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}])

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}4\eta mNL\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty},

where
(a)
holds because of Triangle Inequality,
(b)
holds because of
u(\bm{a},y)\leq 1
,
(c)
holds because of
Lemma

F.2
and
(d)
holds by definition.

By Hoeffding’s inequality, fixing any
h\in\mathcal{H}
, we have with probability
1-\delta
,

{\mathchoice{\left|Z_{h}\right|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{2}{\delta}}{2n}}.

Then as a result of the standard covering number argument we conclude with probability
1-\delta
for any
h\in\mathcal{H}
,

{\mathchoice{\left|Z_{h}\right|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}}\leq 4\eta mNL\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}

where
d_{\infty}(h_{1},h_{2})\triangleq\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}
.
∎

Lemma F.4
.

Fix a class of deterministic predictors
\mathcal{H}
. For any distribution
\mathcal{D}
, let
D\sim\mathcal{D}^{n}
be a dataset consisting of
n
samples
(x^{(i)},y^{(i)})
sampled i.i.d. from
\mathcal{D}
. Then for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability
1-\delta
, for every
f\in\Delta(\mathcal{H}),i\in[N],j\in[d],a_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}
we have

\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}}|}}+{\mathchoice{\left|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}}\right|}{|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}}|}{|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}}|}{|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}]}}|}})}}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}8\eta L\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}

where
(a)
holds because of Triangle Inequality,
(b)
holds because of
y,h(x)\in[-1,1]^{d}
,
(c)
holds because of
Lemma

F.1
and
(d)
holds by definition.

By Hoeffding’s inequality, fixing and
h\in\mathcal{H}
, we have with probability
1-\delta
,

{\mathchoice{\left|Z_{h}\right|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}.

Then as a result of the standard covering number argument we conclude with probability
1-\delta^{\prime}
for any
h\in\mathcal{H}
,

{\mathchoice{\left|Z_{h}\right|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}{|Z_{h}|}}\leq 8\eta L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n}}

where
d_{\infty}(h_{1},h_{2})\triangleq\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}{\mathchoice{\left\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\right\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}{\|h_{1}(x)-h_{2}(x)\|}}_{\infty}
.

Then let
\delta^{\prime}=\delta/{\mathchoice{\left(Ndm\right)}{(Ndm)}{(Ndm)}{(Ndm)}}
, by union bound we have with probability
1-\delta
, for any
h\in\mathcal{H}
,
i\in[N]
and
j\in[d]
and
a_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}
,

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]}{[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]}{[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]}}-\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{D}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]}{[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]}{[{\mathchoice{\left(y_{j}-h(x)_{j}\right)}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}{(y_{j}-h(x)_{j})}}\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]}}\leq 8\eta L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{2\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}}.

∎

Lemma F.5
.

With probability
1-\delta
,

\forall f\in\Delta(\mathcal{H}),\lambda\in\Lambda
,

{\mathchoice{\left|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)\right|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}}\leq\inf_{\epsilon>0}{\mathchoice{\left(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}}\right)}{(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}})}{(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}})}{(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}})}}.

Proof.

This is straightforward from
Lemma

F.3
and
Lemma

F.4
. First we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]+-\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]

\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{a_{i}\in A_{i}}\lambda_{s,i,j,a_{i}}s{\mathchoice{\left(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma\right)}{(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma)}{(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma)}{(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma)}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{s\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{a_{i}\in A_{i}}\lambda_{s,i,j,a_{i}}s{\mathchoice{\left(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma\right)}{(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma)}{(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma)}{(\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot\tilde{b}_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\gamma)}}

Therefore, by union bound with probability
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)\right|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left|\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]-\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]\right|}{|\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]-\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]|}{|\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]-\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]|}{|\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]-\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}u(\bm{a},y)\cdot b(h(x),\bm{a})]|}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in\{+,-\}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{a_{i}\in A_{i}}\lambda_{s,i,j,a_{i}}s{\mathchoice{\left|\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]\right|}{|\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]|}{|\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]|}{|\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]-\mathbb{E}_{f}\mathbb{E}_{D}[(h(x)_{j}-y_{j})\cdot b_{i}(h(x),a_{i})]|}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\inf_{\epsilon>0}{\mathchoice{\left(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}}\right)}{(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}})}{(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}})}{(4\eta(mN+2C)L\epsilon+\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)}{\delta}}{2n}}+C\sqrt{\frac{8\ln\frac{4\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\epsilon)Ndm}{\delta}}{n}})}}.

∎

Now we are ready to prove
Theorem

5.1
.
See
5.1

Proof.

The regret bound of the Hedge algorithm is
O(C\sqrt{T\log Nmd})
, and the ERM oracle gives non-positive regret. From
Lemma

3.1
and
T=O(\log(Nmd)/\epsilon^{4})
, we have that
(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})
is an
{\varepsilon}/4
-approximate equilibrium under
\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f,\lambda)
. From
Lemma

F.5
, we know that when
n>\alpha(\epsilon,\delta,\eta,m,d,N,L)\triangleq O(\ln\frac{\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{H},d_{\infty},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\eta L})Ndm}{\delta}/\epsilon^{4})

with probability
1-\delta
,
\forall f\in\Delta(\mathcal{H}),\lambda\in\Lambda
,

{\mathchoice{\left|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)\right|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}{|\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)|}}\leq\frac{\epsilon}{8}.

Therefore, let
f^{\prime}=\arg\min_{f\in\Delta(\mathcal{H})}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\bar{\lambda})

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f^{\prime},\bar{\lambda})

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})+\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f^{\prime},\bar{\lambda})+\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{D}(f^{\prime},\bar{\lambda})-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(f^{\prime},\bar{\lambda})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{8}+\frac{\epsilon}{4}+\frac{\epsilon}{8}=\frac{\epsilon}{2}.

Similarly, we can prove that

\max_{\lambda\in\Lambda}\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\hat{f},\lambda)-\tilde{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}.

Therefore,
(\hat{f},\bar{\lambda})
is an
\epsilon/2
-approximate equilibrium for the payoff
\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(f,\lambda)
under the true distribution
\mathcal{D}
. Then, similar to
Lemma

3.2
, we have

\mathrm{DecCE}(\hat{f})\leq\gamma+\frac{1+\epsilon}{C}\leq\gamma+\frac{1+\epsilon}{2/\epsilon}\leq\gamma+\frac{2}{2/\epsilon}=\gamma+\epsilon,

and

\mathbb{E}_{h\sim f}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}[u(\bm{a},y)\cdot\tilde{b}(h(x),\bm{a})]\geq\mathrm{OPT}(\mathcal{H},\mathcal{D},\gamma)-\epsilon.

Finally by
Theorem

C.3
, the receivers who play
\eta
-quantal response obtain the stated swap regret bound.
∎
